With 3a and 3b the badged-endpoint and signal-slot tables are fully in frames [withdrawn 2026-11-10: guards and index still heap, gap U14]; still in kernel tables: the task row and inbox (unit 1), CNode capability nodes (arena 2b-2d), IO spaces, EPTs, VCPUs, `objs` headers (unit 4), and the mapping tables. Strength: HOST. **F6/F7 (2026-11-04): CAPABILITY RECORDS ARE IN THE CNODE'S OWN FRAMES.** A capability is no longer a guarded node in a kernel table: it is a 128-byte record in the slot of the CNode it sits in (`cspace/caprec.rs`, `framenodes.rs`, `linktree.rs`), found by the id's location ([stamp 42, cn_idx 14, slot 8]) and accepted only on an equal stamp. A record is nine data words (kind and rights, object, badge, parent, first child, next sibling, previous sibling, object + 1, own id), ten CRC-32C check words bound to the SLOT'S LOCATION, an XOR parity word and one zero word. The derivation tree is intrusive links (parent, first child, a circular sibling ring, the youngest child is first.prev); the per-node `children` Vec, `NodeStore` and the `CapNode` heap map are gone. A Move changes the slot, so it gives the capability a NEW id and rekeys every reference to it (`tests/cap_move_ids.rs`). SELF-HEAL, not lost: a read verifies the record and a record that fails reads as ABSENT (fail closed); one bad data word is rebuilt from the parity and the others (and the rebuilt word must itself verify), a bad check word is rewritten when the data agree with the parity, data and check word changed together or two bad words are UNREPAIRABLE and left as found, and the scrubber walks every record of every CNode (`cap_heal_node`, containment of an unrepairable record withdraws its subtree by the parent words). EVIDENCE (HOST, counting allocator and the workspace tests): (1) DIFFERENTIAL against a heap reference model, every result and the full state of every slot compared after EVERY operation: `capstore_differential.rs` 3,000 sequences, 42,000 operations (install, copy, mint, move, mutate, delete, revoke step; per-kind effect counts printed), and `capstore_differential2.rs` 31,000 operations over retype (counts 1 to 3 frames, a range past the CNode, an occupied destination, an exhausted 4-frame region), the untyped's revoke to completion, and the by-pointer forms `cs_copy`, `cs_mint`, `cs_move`, `cs_mutate`, `cs_delete`, `cs_revoke_step` through a task's CSpace root with two-level pointers into a second CNode; effect counts (took effect / run): copy 1,444 / 3,061, mint 276 / 2,093, move 1,022 / 2,123, mutate 459 / 2,166, delete 1,036 / 2,105, revoke 1,059 / 2,109, retype 2,106 / 3,101 (class A) and 3,820 / 7,463 (class B), untyped revoke 1,011 and 2,537; NO retype refusal disagreed with the model (no finding); the retyped objects are checked distinct (the model adopts the kernel's choice of object ids). (2) IN-FRAME CORRUPTION: every single-bit flip of every word of every record is detected and repaired back to the original; a double flip reads as absent and is never a wrong record; a link word flipped is repaired and the tree is the same tree (`selfheal_caps.rs`, `caprec.rs`, `framenodes.rs` unit tests). (3) PLANTS, each turned RED by a named test: `plants_cap_inframe.py` 8 of 8 (read unchecked, restore wrong, healing disabled, no containment, scrubber skips CNodes, delete drops the children, a Move keeps the old reference, retype installs each child one slot past the one asked for: RED on the slot comparison itself, `slot (0,1) differs`), `plants_framenodes.py` 5 of 5, `plants_caprec.py` 5 of 5, `plants_linktree.py` 4 of 4, `plants_heap_growth.py` 6 of 6, `plants_heap_charge.py` 4 of 4. (4) HEAP GROWTH, DERIVED: the capability record (128 B) is now in the frames the CNode's retype charged; what the heap still holds per capability is the id index entry, MEASURED at 25 B at the least and 42 B at the most (`tests/heap_growth.rs`), so `CAP_NODE_BYTES` falls from 896 to 48 (at least half of it is measured, at most all: 25 >= 24, 42 <= 48) and a slot is charged `CNODE_SLOT_BYTES` = 40 (empty slot) + 48 + 128 (the record) = 216 B. `cnode_frames`: 1 frame up to 16 slots, 2 at 32, 4 at 64, 7 at 128, 14 at 256 (the previous charge was 1 frame up to 4 slots, 2 at 8, 4 at 16, 8 at 32, 15 at 64, 30 at 128 and 59 at 256: a CNode of 256 slots takes 14 frames instead of 59). Measured, filled by fan-out, by a derivation chain and by badged mints: 256 slots keep 15,772 B of kernel heap against 57,344 B charged (16 slots: 2,332 B of 4,096 B), at every size within the charge, and the charge is not slack (a plant of a charge 6 times too large is RED). Strength: HOST (the differential is against a model, not a proof; not model-checked and not run on silicon). **F6/F7 (2026-11-05, a queued submission): THE CNODE'S HEADER AND SLOTS ARE IN ITS OWN FRAMES.** There is no slot array and no `CnMap` any more. A CNode's frames start with one 128-byte HEADER block (the same codec as a record: CRC-32C check words bound to the place `1<<40 + cn_idx`, an XOR parity word) holding its radix, guard, guard bits and its own id; slot `i` is the record block at `base + 128 + i*128`, occupied exactly when the block is not all zero (a record that fails its check is still an OCCUPIED slot: the scrubber heals or contains it, it is never read as empty). Heal and containment work BY PLACE (a record whose own id word is the damaged one is found and, when unrepairable, withdrawn with the subtree using the ids its verifying neighbours hold for that place). A CNode whose header the object memory refuses is not created (retype refused). The index (id -> slot) and the guards stay in the kernel table. The repair and the consistency test are the `GMap` functions themselves (`parts_consistent`, `parts_reseal`, `heal_parts`; a child guard is re-sealed in place by `GuardedVec::reload`, so no destructor), only the store differs. A frame word that is flipped, or that no field uses, makes the record read as ABSENT (fail closed) and the scrubber (implemented and tested, not yet run on a timer in the released image) rewrites the slot from the guards; a new context into a full table is refused (`Exhausted`); a destroyed context's slot is zeroed. Evidence: `framemap.rs` differential test against `GMap` (40,000 random operations, the full-table refusal exercised), every frame word and sampled bits of a slot flipped and repaired, a removed record leaves a zero slot; `synthyrion_kheal/tests/reload.rs`; plants RED (read without the consistency check, remove without the scrub, a refill dropped when stored, reload without the parity check); microkernel suite plain and fault_injection green. HOST only. **F6/F7-h item 1 (2026-11-01): a RETYPED context's record is IN the frame its region was charged for it**: `retype_in_for` places the 32 words at the first byte of the charged frame (`FMap::insert_at`, the address `FRAME_ARENA_BASE + start * FRAME_SIZE` through `real_phys`, as a notification's), so the charge HOLDS the state. A placement over another record's slot (two charges for one frame), over the table's own reserved block, for an id already present, or into a full table is REFUSED with nothing written (`Invalid` / `Exhausted`); destroying the context (its last capability, or the revoke of its region) scrubs all 32 words, and a store covers the whole slot, so nothing an earlier holder left survives. A frame corrupted behind the kernel's back reads as ABSENT (fail closed) and the scrubber repairs it from the guards; corrupting one context's frame does not reach another's. Evidence: `tests/sc_in_frame.rs` (3: the record read from the attached RAM at the charged frame over garbage, a flipped word not believed, the binding at word 4, the revoke leaving the frame zero, two contexts in two frames, a heap-created context in the reserved block), `framemap.rs` placement unit test; plants `plants_sc_in_frame.py` 6 of 6 RED (the record one frame away, every context at the first frame, no scrub on remove, a store that skips zero words, the overlap test dropped, a placement inside the reserved block accepted). HOST only. since F6/F7 gap 1 (2026-11-03) it takes ONE FRAME FROM THE FRAME ALLOCATOR (counted in use like any other, as `create_root_untyped` carves its region from it) and keeps its record there, no longer in a reserved block (the 4-frame reserved block is gone: `SHARE_TABLE_FRAMES` is the share table's frames alone); no allocator frame left is `Exhausted` and nothing is created; the frame is given back, scrubbed, only when the scrubber CONTAINS the record (a heap-created context has no destroy path, so it lives for the kernel's life otherwise); tests `sc_in_frame.rs` (the record at the first bytes of its allocator frame, a corrupted frame not believed, `frames_in_use` +1, the exhausted allocator) and `selfheal_aux.rs` (a contained heap-created context returns its frame); plants RED (`plants_sc_in_frame.py`: no allocator frame taken, the wrong refusal, the frame leaked on containment). `create_sched_context` itself stays callable until the seal; the guards (two check-word tables per context) and the id-to-frame index stay in the kernel's own storage, not in the frame. **F6/F7 item 2 (2026-11-02): that storage is now ALLOCATED ONCE AT CREATION and never grows** (the epoch table's pattern, `epochs.rs`): the index is a sorted array of `(id, pool slot)` pairs in two boxed arrays of `MAX_SCHED_CONTEXTS` entries, searched by a COUNTED binary search of at most `FMap::PROBES` (`floor(log2 CAP) + 1` = 7) probes, with the insert shifting up and the remove shifting down in COUNTED loops of at most CAP iterations; the guards are a pool of CAP records whose child-guard tables are created with the capacity of a context's refill array (`FrameRec::CHILD_WORDS`, `GuardedVec::with_capacity`), so re-sealing a refill list (`GuardedVec::reload`) never allocates. No `BTreeMap`, and no per-context allocation after creation, in the table. Evidence: `framemap.rs` tests (the same differential test against `GMap` still passes over the new index; `the_index_is_allocated_once_stays_sorted_and_never_grows`: 40,000 random inserts, removals and child-list re-seals with the storage signature (index length, pool length, total child-table capacity) identical to creation at every step, the index strictly ascending with every entry on a distinct used pool slot, `next_id` walking the live ids in order); `synthyrion_kheal/tests/reload.rs` (a table created for 16 words never reallocates through 2,000 reloads of 0 to 16 words); plants RED (`plants_sc_in_frame.py`: the search misses the last element, a remove leaves the tail unshifted, an insert does not shift, the child tables created with no reservation). HOST only. **Still open:** the WCET table does not yet show these paths BOUNDED (the generator stops earlier, at the object-memory emulation, F29), and the guards are allocated at creation but are heap memory, not frames. **F6/F7 item 3 (2026-11-03): the loop-bound rows for the counted loops are written** (`wcet_loop_bounds.tsv`: `FMap::find` over `SC_INDEX_PROBES` = 7, the pool scans, shifts and `members_where` over `MAX_SCHED_CONTEXTS` = 64, slot load / store / scrub / write-back over `SLOT_WORDS` = 32, `Sc::set_refill_words` and the slot codec over `SC_REFILL_WORDS` = 16 and `SC_W_REFILLS` = 8; `wcet_constants.tsv` pins each name to a literal `const` in its source file, with const asserts tying `SC_INDEX_PROBES` and `SC_REFILL_WORDS` to their formulas; three callee rows for integer conversions and `abs_diff`). Measured by `synthyrion_wcetan` (bounds are MIR-statement counts from the host analysis, not cycles on hardware): BOUNDED now `FMap::find` 580, `next_id` 628, `overlaps_any` 3,663, `take` 8,611, `Sc::set_refill_words` 979, `Sc::scalar_words` 49, `set_scalar_words` 80, the slot codec `to_words` 1,715 and `from_words` 5,414 (the `Sc` scalar words were moved to uniquely named helpers because the analysis keys functions by readable name and the object header\'s `scalars` shared the name; the codec lost its `map_or` / `unwrap_or` / `min` combinators for `match`). A refill list longer than the array is REFUSED and nothing changes (the self-heal repair writes child lists back through `Rec::set_children`, which replaced `children_mut`; a guarded list that does not fit is a `Conflict`, never truncated); unit test + `selfheal_aux.rs` green on the old assertions; plants `plants_sc_fixed.py` 2 of 2 RED; HOST only; **F6/F7-g (2026-10-29): NOTIFICATIONS keep their state IN THEIR CHARGED FRAME.** The pending word, the bound task and the waiter queue's head and tail are four words at the start of the notification's own frame, read and written through an object-memory window (`objmem.rs`: the existing `PhysMem` trait the arch layer implements, or the host emulation when none is attached; `attach_object_memory` only before any notification exists and before the seal; a refused access is counted, `object_memory_faults`); the waiter queue is linked through the waiting tasks, so leaving it is O(1) and `forget_task_ipcx` no longer walks every notification, and `destroy_notification` walks the queue instead of the task table; the frame is zeroed when the notification is made and scrubbed when it is destroyed; the kernel keeps one index entry (notification id -> frame). `notification_invariants` checks every queue against the tasks. `tests/ntfn_in_frame.rs` (3) attaches its own RAM and reads and writes the frame behind the kernel's back; `heap_charge.rs` measures 572 B of live kernel heap per notification against 543 B per plain frame object (the region bookkeeping), the difference being the index entry; plants `plants_ntfn_in_frame.py` 7 of 7 RED. A behaviour change: a task that waits on a second notification leaves the first queue (the old lists kept a stale entry that a later signal woke spuriously), and a waiter of a destroyed notification now runs again (it was woken while still marked waiting, so it was never dispatched again; tested). Still in kernel tables, stated at the mechanism: the task row and inbox, scheduling contexts (guarded `GMap`, self-heal), CNode slots, IO spaces, EPTs, VCPUs, and every object's header in `objs`; **F6/F7-d (2026-10-21): ANONYMOUS MEMORY names its region.** `map_anonymous_from(task, ut, virt, len)` and `cs_map_anonymous(task, ut_ptr, virt, len)` map fresh, zeroed, writable, never-executable pages whose frames are retyped Frame objects charged to the named untyped (all or nothing; a refusal, including one after the first chunk of frames was taken, maps and charges nothing; the region needs WRITE; revoking the region unmaps every page and frees every frame); `seal_object_creation` now closes `MapMemory` mode 0 (anonymous) and mode 3 (file; the page cache took allocator frames uncharged), so after the seal no frame leaves the allocator except through a region; and a request is at most `MAX_MAP_PAGES` (1024) pages: `len` comes from ring 3 and the range check and frame reservation were O(len) (a 2^40-page request was a ring-0 denial of service). 3 tests, 9 plants RED. **F6/F7-c (2026-10-21): REPLY SLOTS are part of their caller's task object.** A task has at most ONE outstanding call or fault (a call needs an idle, free-to-run caller; a fault needs a task that is not held; both hold it), so a reply slot is a fixed-size field of its caller, `TASK_OBJECT_BYTES` counts one `ReplySlot` per task and `ipc_invariants` states the bound (no caller has two slots, every slot belongs to a live held task, the table is never longer than the task table); an exhaustive sweep of 100,000 call / receive / reply / fault / restart / exit sequences over three tasks checks it after every operation and FOUND a real defect: `call` accepted a caller held by a fault, so a fault-suspended task could hold two slots (fixed: a caller must be free to run). the per-caller slot gives the same bound without changing the proved IPC model (see F13b). 2 tests, 4 plants RED. **F6/F7-b (2026-10-21): badged-endpoint table entries and signal slots are retyped too** (`retype_badged_endpoint(ut, from, badge, rights)`: the authority is a genuine UNBADGED source endpoint capability with GRANT, the new capability never exceeds the source's rights, a second retype of the same pair is a second object; `retype_signal_slot(ut, notif_obj, badge)`: the authority is WRITE on the notification OBJECT; each charged one frame, bounded by `MAX_BADGED_ENDPOINTS` / `MAX_SIGNAL_SLOTS` as a named `Exhausted`, destroyed with the region (the capability is then dead); a plain `retype` / `cs_retype` of the two kinds is refused because it could name any target; `seal_object_creation` closes `mint_badged_endpoint` and `sealed_signal_cap`; slot-path badged capabilities (`cs_mint`) are CapNodes accounted by their CNode and need no table; 4 tests, 12 plants RED) **F6/F7-a2 (2026-10-21): the task INBOX is charged with the task object**: it was an unbounded `VecDeque`, so the one frame a retyped task was charged covered nothing; now it has `INBOX_CAP` (32) incoming slots plus `MAX_OUTSTANDING_SENDS` (32) reserved bounce slots, is allocated once at `INBOX_SLOTS` and never grows, a full inbox REFUSES, a sender may have at most `MAX_OUTSTANDING_SENDS` word messages outstanding so every bounce finds the slot reserved for it, and a retyped TASK object is charged `TASK_OBJECT_FRAMES` (4: its row and its whole inbox at the largest delivery size, asserted by a test) instead of 1; 7 tests, 10 plants RED **F6/F7-f (2026-10-25): the three remaining heap-born OBJECT kinds are retyped and the rule is a census test.** A scan of every insertion into an object table found IO spaces (`iospace_create`), EPT objects (`ept_create`) and VCPUs made by `vcpu_create_on` created from the kernel heap. `RetypeKind::IoSpace` (`retype_iospace`, needs the IOMMU authority) and `RetypeKind::Ept` (`retype_ept`, needs the hypervisor authority) charge ONE frame, are bounded by `MAX_IOSPACES` / `MAX_EPTS` as a named `Exhausted`, are refused by a plain `retype` / `cs_retype` (they could not show the authority), and are destroyed with their region (an IO space: mappings unmapped, devices back to deny, backend space destroyed; an EPT: guest mappings gone, every VCPU bound to it Dead, epoch slot freed; both capabilities dead); `iospace_create`, `ept_create` and `vcpu_create_on` are closed by `seal_object_creation` (a VCPU is then retype-only: `RetypeKind::Vcpu` + `vcpu_bind`). `tests/f7_retype_only.rs`: 7 tests, and a CENSUS (source scan, TESTED not proved) that every insertion into an object table (`scs`, `notifs`, `badged`, `signals`, `rings`, `vcpus`, `epts`, io `spaces`, `asid_pools`, `cnodes`, `tasks`) sits in a function of a table in which each creator is either not `pub` (reachable only from retype) or consults `creation_sealed`, with stale-row and planted-creator convictions; 5 feature plants (`plant_f7_iospace_unsealed`, `_iospace_nodestroy`, `_ept_unsealed`, `_ept_nodestroy`, `_vcpu_unsealed`) each RED, and one planted in the real source and restored. NOT covered, stated at the mechanism: (1) the objects' STATE still lives in kernel `BTreeMap`s that the charged frame accounts for; (2) ADDRESS SPACES (`synthyrion_vmm::AddressSpace`, created by the L1 sandbox and held in `Spaces::by_root`) are not retyped objects; (3) reply slots are fields of the caller's task object (F6/F7-c) and ring re-insertions are not creations. Object-map capacities for the F29 work stream: `MAX_IOSPACES` (16), `MAX_EPTS` (16), `MAX_VCPUS`, `MAX_SCHED_CONTEXTS`, `MAX_BADGED_ENDPOINTS`, `MAX_SIGNAL_SLOTS`, task rows by `TASK_OBJECT_FRAMES` (4 frames each), every other kind one frame. **F6/F7 gap 6 and the HAVE statement (2026-11-03).** (1) THE CHARGE NOW BOUNDS THE KERNEL HEAP FOR A FILLED CNODE (a real defect, found by measuring). F6/F7-f measured every kind retyped to exhaustion, but a CNode only EMPTY (its slots charged 40 B each). A CNode of 256 slots filled by copying one capability into every slot kept 166,700 B of kernel heap (653 B per occupied slot; 542 B at 16 slots, 572 B at 64) against the 12,288 B it was charged: 13.6 times what the region paid, and 14 to 15 times in the worst fill (a derivation chain, 720 B per slot, 192,732 B in all). The claim "the charge accounts for the heap" was FALSE for capability copies, derivations and mints, the operations that fill a CNode: a user holding one CNode could make the kernel hold far more than the user's memory paid for. Every slot is now charged FULL: `CAP_NODE_BYTES` 896 + `CNODE_EMPTY_SLOT_BYTES` 40 = `CNODE_SLOT_BYTES` 936, `cnode_frames` 59 at 256 slots [SUPERSEDED 2026-11-04 by the capability records in the CNode frames: now 48 + 40 + 128 = 216 per slot, 14 frames at 256; see below]. COST, stated: a CNode takes about 20 times the frames at 256 slots (59 against 3) and 4 times at 16 slots; the boot root CNode (64 slots, 15 frames) is kept back in the LARGEST boot region (`ROOT_CNODE_SLACK_FRAMES` = `cnode_frames(CNODE_MAX_BITS)` = 59 frames, `root_cnode_region`, `region_untyped_frames`) instead of one frame in every region; both live boots (x86 UEFI, AArch64) call `region_untyped_frames`. THE CHARGE MAY ONLY DROP LATER with a measured filled-CNode run proving held <= charged; the F29 U2 node-store move (capability records kept in the CNode's own slots) is the path to that, and `tests/heap_growth.rs` turns RED on purpose the moment capabilities become cheaper than half the constant, so the constant is lowered in the same unit that proves it. (2) EVIDENCE (HOST, the workspace's counting allocator): `tests/heap_growth.rs` fills CNodes of 4 to 256 slots by copy fan-out, by a derivation chain and by badged mints: the heap the retype and the fill leave live is at most the frames charged at every size (the tightest, 16 slots by chain, is 14,844 B of 16,384 B); the per-capability cost is at most `CAP_NODE_BYTES` and, for the chain, at least half of it (the charge is not slack); deleting every capability and refilling three times does not grow the heap; a task's inbox is allocated once, so sending until it is full and being refused allocates nothing; `bootmem.rs` boots a root CNode of 16, 64 and 256 slots with the slack `region_untyped_frames` keeps back and shows the old one-frame rule refused (`Exhausted`); Plants RED by name: `plants_heap_growth.py` (the old 40 B charge, `cnode_frames` ignoring the filled slots, a charge 6 times too large, a delete that leaves its node, an inbox that grows as messages queue, the old one-frame boot slack); `plants_heap_charge.py` re-pointed at the new constants. BOOT CHECKED IN QEMU TCG (VM, not bare metal): x86 UEFI under QEMU TCG, normal scenes: the default image (f17) 20/20, bootarm / BOOTROOT 23/23, bootinfo 12/12, gate -smp 1 65/65; AArch64 fast proof PASS (EL1 and EL2); the full AArch64 scenario sweep ran 25 min with no panic but did not complete, so there is no full-sweep verdict. (3) WHAT F6/F7 ARE, at HOST strength, and what is NOT shown. Held, measured: every kernel object after `seal_object_creation` is a retype charged to a region (model-checked, `synthyrion_kmath::untyped`); the kernel heap held for each retyped kind, for a filled CNode and for queued messages is at most the memory charged, and the revoke gives it back; reused memory is zeroed (obligation 2, model-checked); notifications and scheduling contexts keep their state in memory their region was charged for (a retyped scheduling context in its own frame, a heap-created one in one allocator frame), reply slots and the inbox are fixed parts of the task object. THE GAPS ARE EXECUTABLE (stage 1a, work item heap1_stage1a): `tests/heap_born_census.rs` holds one gate per kind; `a_task_is_not_heap_born` (a retyped task keeps 19,490 B of kernel heap against a 1,024 B index-entry budget) is `#[ignore]`d and UN-IGNORED BY proper (task row and inbox in the task object's frames), `a_cnode_is_not_heap_born` (a retyped 16-slot CNode keeps 1,486 B) BY the CapNode arena stages 2b and 2d; both print their measured number with `--ignored --nocapture`, and the notification gate is live. (b) the guards and the id index of the scheduling-context table are allocated once at creation in kernel memory, not in the frame; (c) in the live boots no `PhysMem` window is attached to the kernel (`attach_object_memory`), so notification and scheduling-context state sits in the object-memory emulation (kernel memory) there, and the in-frame property is host-tested with an attached window only; a VM scene that attaches an identity window over the kernel's regions and reads a retyped frame back is the missing evidence (it also needs the arch layer's `unsafe` window, the unsafe-confinement record and the boot-stack gate, and a fault in it breaks the default boot, so it is not done in a release-day unit); (d) heap growth from operations on tables other than the ones measured here (frame mappings, the page-table model, shares) is bounded by their own limits and is NOT measured against a charge; (e) **CORRECTED 2026-11-10: this sentence is no longer true.** The host object-memory emulation is a bounded array (`objmem::Emu`, EMU_WORDS 29,968 words, a binary search of EMU_PROBES 15 probes) with loop rows for `Emu::lower` and the `Emu::set` insert and remove shifts in `docs/kernel/wcet/wcet_loop_bounds.tsv`, and the generated `docs/kernel/KERNEL_WCET_TABLE.md` on main d978cae93 names `ObjRam::peek` / `write` as the first thing in the way of NO entry (its root causes are now `AsidPool::new`, `<usize as From<u16>>::from`, `BTreeSet::contains`, `BTreeMap::remove` / `range`, `RingMem::read`, `Iterator::enumerate` and similar); `synthyrion_wcetan check` reports 0 problems with the table current (the stage of a queued submission ACCEPTED it in 991 s). What is STILL open in the WCET table is exactly the 29 `PENDING` kernel entries for those other reasons (unbounded maps, allocation, loops with no capacity), among them `retype`, `cnode_copy`, `cnode_lookup` and the two scrubbers `selfheal_Kernel__cap_scrub` / `obj_scrub` (their loops now have a source-constant ceiling, below, but their per-unit lookups are over tables with no enforced capacity). Strength: HOST static analysis, in MIR statements, not cycles. LOCAL SELF-CHECKS RUN before the direct enqueue: the microkernel suite with `fault_injection` at green; `synthyrion_gatecases` and `synthyrion_vmhost` green; plants 27 of 27 RED (`plants_heap_growth.py` 6, `plants_sc_gap.py` 5, `plants_sc_in_frame.py` 12, `plants_heap_charge.py` 4) and `plants_sc_rows.py` 5 of 5 earlier; NOT run locally: the census-check and the full `synthyrion_kmath` suite on the final tree (its census test is regenerated at landing). **F6/F7 (2026-11-04): BADGED-ENDPOINT ENTRIES ARE IN THEIR CHARGED FRAMES.** The badged-endpoint table is no longer a kernel `BTreeMap`: it is `FMap<PairRec, MAX_BADGED_ENDPOINTS>` (`pairrec.rs`, `framemap.rs`), the fixed-capacity guarded frame table the scheduling contexts use. A retyped badged endpoint keeps its two words (target task, badge) at the first bytes of the frame its retype charged (the rest of the slot is written zero, so no word of an earlier holder survives); one made before the seal takes ONE allocator frame it owns and gives back, scrubbed, when the entry goes; the table's index and guards are allocated once at kernel creation. The withdrawal (`revoke_badged_of`) finds a target's entries by the target word (the frame's, or the guarded copy's when the frame is damaged) into a fixed list and removes them without checking, so a damaged record cannot keep an endpoint alive; a corrupted record reads as absent (fail closed). MEASURED (the workspace counting allocator, HOST): 256 heap-created endpoints leave 0 B of kernel heap live; 256 retyped endpoints then revoked leave 0 B on the second round (5,120 B once, the object tables' first growth). Tests: `badged_in_frame.rs` (5), the codec tests in `pairrec.rs` (2), a differential test that the frame table's consistency test equals the generic one; 10 plants RED (`plants_badged_in_frame.py`). WCET: `FMap::get` is costed, so `send_caps` stays BOUNDED (1,999,967 -> 2,066,277 MIR statements, one frame-table get), `synthyrion_wcetan check` 0 problems; the frame table's rows are stated at its largest instantiation (256 entries, 9 probes, 16 child words), and the table's own consistency test is a bounded function because the generic one walks an object header's unbounded child list (114 M statements). Not yet in frames: signal slots (unit 3b, queued), the task row and inbox, CNode capability nodes (arena 2a-2d), IO spaces, EPTs, VCPUs, `objs` headers. Strength: HOST. Local self-checks: the full microkernel suite with `fault_injection` (84 test binaries; its one failure, the stack-frame budget of `mint_badged_endpoint` and `revoke_object_caps`, is fixed and `stack_frames` is green), plants 10 of 10 RED. **F6/F7 (2026-11-04): SIGNAL SLOTS ARE IN THEIR CHARGED FRAMES.** The signal-slot table is no longer a kernel `BTreeMap`: it is `FMap<PairRec, MAX_SIGNAL_SLOTS>`, the table of unit 3a with its own check-word domain. A retyped slot keeps its two words (notification, badge) at the first bytes of the frame its retype charged; one made before the seal (`sealed_signal_cap`) takes ONE allocator frame it owns and gives back, scrubbed, with the slot. A notification's death takes every slot that raises it (found by the notification word into a caller-owned fixed list, in a non-inlined function, so no caller's stack frame grows) and the interrupt lines that signalled through them; a corrupted record reads as absent (fail closed). MEASURED (counting allocator, HOST): 256 heap-created slots leave 0 B of kernel heap live; 256 retyped slots then revoked leave 0 B on the second round. Tests: `signal_in_frame.rs` (5); 8 plants RED (`plants_signal_in_frame.py`); the table, codec and consistency-test plants of 3a cover the shared code. WCET: `synthyrion_wcetan check` 0 problems (new constants MAX_SIGNAL_SLOTS and EMU_WORDS 24,576, loop rows for `IdList::contains` and the notification-death walk). With 3a and 3b the badged-endpoint and signal-slot tables are fully in frames [withdrawn 2026-11-10: guards and index still heap, gap U14]; still in kernel tables: the task row and inbox (unit 1), CNode capability nodes (arena 2b-2d), IO spaces, EPTs, VCPUs, `objs` headers (unit 4), and the mapping tables. Strength: HOST. **F6/F7 (2026-11-04): CAPABILITY RECORDS ARE IN THE CNODE'S OWN FRAMES.** A capability is no longer a guarded node in a kernel table: it is a 128-byte record in the slot of the CNode it sits in (`cspace/caprec.rs`, `framenodes.rs`, `linktree.rs`), found by the id's location ([stamp 42, cn_idx 14, slot 8]) and accepted only on an equal stamp. A record is nine data words (kind and rights, object, badge, parent, first child, next sibling, previous sibling, object + 1, own id), ten CRC-32C check words bound to the SLOT'S LOCATION, an XOR parity word and one zero word. The derivation tree is intrusive links (parent, first child, a circular sibling ring, the youngest child is first.prev); the per-node `children` Vec, `NodeStore` and the `CapNode` heap map are gone. A Move changes the slot, so it gives the capability a NEW id and rekeys every reference to it (`tests/cap_move_ids.rs`). SELF-HEAL, not lost: a read verifies the record and a record that fails reads as ABSENT (fail closed); one bad data word is rebuilt from the parity and the others (and the rebuilt word must itself verify), a bad check word is rewritten when the data agree with the parity, data and check word changed together or two bad words are UNREPAIRABLE and left as found, and the scrubber walks every record of every CNode (`cap_heal_node`, containment of an unrepairable record withdraws its subtree by the parent words). EVIDENCE (HOST, counting allocator and the workspace tests): (1) DIFFERENTIAL against a heap reference model, every result and the full state of every slot compared after EVERY operation: `capstore_differential.rs` 3,000 sequences, 42,000 operations (install, copy, mint, move, mutate, delete, revoke step; per-kind effect counts printed), and `capstore_differential2.rs` 31,000 operations over retype (counts 1 to 3 frames, a range past the CNode, an occupied destination, an exhausted 4-frame region), the untyped's revoke to completion, and the by-pointer forms `cs_copy`, `cs_mint`, `cs_move`, `cs_mutate`, `cs_delete`, `cs_revoke_step` through a task's CSpace root with two-level pointers into a second CNode; effect counts (took effect / run): copy 1,444 / 3,061, mint 276 / 2,093, move 1,022 / 2,123, mutate 459 / 2,166, delete 1,036 / 2,105, revoke 1,059 / 2,109, retype 2,106 / 3,101 (class A) and 3,820 / 7,463 (class B), untyped revoke 1,011 and 2,537; NO retype refusal disagreed with the model (no finding); the retyped objects are checked distinct (the model adopts the kernel's choice of object ids). (2) IN-FRAME CORRUPTION: every single-bit flip of every word of every record is detected and repaired back to the original; a double flip reads as absent and is never a wrong record; a link word flipped is repaired and the tree is the same tree (`selfheal_caps.rs`, `caprec.rs`, `framenodes.rs` unit tests). (3) PLANTS, each turned RED by a named test: `plants_cap_inframe.py` 8 of 8 (read unchecked, restore wrong, healing disabled, no containment, scrubber skips CNodes, delete drops the children, a Move keeps the old reference, retype installs each child one slot past the one asked for: RED on the slot comparison itself, `slot (0,1) differs`), `plants_framenodes.py` 5 of 5, `plants_caprec.py` 5 of 5, `plants_linktree.py` 4 of 4, `plants_heap_growth.py` 6 of 6, `plants_heap_charge.py` 4 of 4. (4) HEAP GROWTH, DERIVED: the capability record (128 B) is now in the frames the CNode's retype charged; what the heap still holds per capability is the id index entry, MEASURED at 25 B at the least and 42 B at the most (`tests/heap_growth.rs`), so `CAP_NODE_BYTES` falls from 896 to 48 (at least half of it is measured, at most all: 25 >= 24, 42 <= 48) and a slot is charged `CNODE_SLOT_BYTES` = 40 (empty slot) + 48 + 128 (the record) = 216 B. `cnode_frames`: 1 frame up to 16 slots, 2 at 32, 4 at 64, 7 at 128, 14 at 256 (the previous charge was 1 frame up to 4 slots, 2 at 8, 4 at 16, 8 at 32, 15 at 64, 30 at 128 and 59 at 256: a CNode of 256 slots takes 14 frames instead of 59). Measured, filled by fan-out, by a derivation chain and by badged mints: 256 slots keep 15,772 B of kernel heap against 57,344 B charged (16 slots: 2,332 B of 4,096 B), at every size within the charge, and the charge is not slack (a plant of a charge 6 times too large is RED). (5) `synthyrion_wcetan check` 0 problems (`check_word` costed as a derived callee; the stale `CapNode` rows removed). The kernel-math census classifies the new functions at landing. [SUPERSEDED by below] STILL IN KERNEL TABLES, stated at the mechanism: the CNode's SLOT ARRAY and its per-CNode structures (an EMPTY retyped 16-slot CNode still keeps 1,486 B of kernel heap: `a_cnode_is_not_heap_born` stays `#[ignore]`d, un-ignored by unit 2c, the slot array in the CNode's frames), the id index entry per capability (25 to 42 B, charged), the task row and inbox (unit 1), IO spaces, EPTs, VCPUs, `objs` headers (unit 4), the scheduling-context guards (unit 5) and the mapping tables. Strength: HOST (the differential is against a model, not a proof; not model-checked and not run on silicon). **F6/F7 (2026-11-05, a queued submission): THE CNODE'S HEADER AND SLOTS ARE IN ITS OWN FRAMES.** There is no slot array and no `CnMap` any more. A CNode's frames start with one 128-byte HEADER block (the same codec as a record: CRC-32C check words bound to the place `1<<40 + cn_idx`, an XOR parity word) holding its radix, guard, guard bits and its own id; slot `i` is the record block at `base + 128 + i*128`, occupied exactly when the block is not all zero (a record that fails its check is still an OCCUPIED slot: the scrubber heals or contains it, it is never read as empty). Heal and containment work BY PLACE (a record whose own id word is the damaged one is found and, when unrepairable, withdrawn with the subtree using the ids its verifying neighbours hold for that place). A CNode whose header the object memory refuses is not created (retype refused). ## §  Evidence

The source table cites code locations, test names and measurement runs for every row. Those citations are internal to the project’s repository and are not reproduced here. The download carries the source and the checking evidence that exists today (see [Download](https://synthyrion.com/core/downloads.html)).
